==== dump ast bench/compare/fung/json.fung
-- stdout
| (program (import fs) (import json) (assign rows (unwrap ! (call (field parse json) (unwrap ! (call (field read fs) "data/data.json"))))) (assign ids (call fold rows 0 (fn (s, r) (block (then (field active r) (binary + s (field id r)) s))))) (assign tags (call fold rows 0 (fn (s, r) (block (binary + s (call len (field tags r))))))) (call print ids tags (binary > (call len (call (field stringify json) rows)) 1000000)))
-- stderr
-- exit: 0
==== dump core bench/compare/fung/json.fung
-- stdout

(call (field read (global 0)) "data/data.json"))))) (let (global 3) (call (prelude fold) (global 2) 0 (fn (s, r) (do (if (field active (local 1)) (op + (local 0) (field id (local 1))) (local 0)))))) (let (global 4) (call (prelude fold) (global 2) 0 (fn (s, r) (do (op + (local 0) (call (prelude len) (field tags (local 1)))))))) (call (prelude print) (global 3) (global 4) (op > (call (prelude len) (call (field stringify (global 1)) (global 2))) 1000000)))

==== dump ast bench/compare/fung/list.fung
-- stdout
| (program (assign xs (call map (range .. 0 500000) (fn (i) (block (binary % (binary * i 7) 1000))))) (assign ys (call filter (call map xs (fn (x) (block (binary + x 1)))) (fn (x) (block (binary == (binary % x 2) 0))))) (assign zs (call sort_by xs (fn (x) (block (unary - x))))) (call print (binary + (binary + (call sum ys) (index zs 0)) (call len ys))))
